```
g = cp.Variable(n)
cons = [g >=0]
# compute convolution constraint (f*g) <= M. Use cp? We can construct Toeplitz matrix A @ g <= M.
# A is (2n-1) x n. For n=1000, 2M floats. OK.
# A[k,j] = f[j] if 0 <= k-j < n else 0. Equivalent A[k] = f[k-j] for j in range.
# Build A efficiently: for k, start=max(0,k-n+1), end=min(n-1,k)+1; segment = f[end-1-start:-1:-1]? Let's derive: For fixed k, g_j contributes f[k-j]. j range. As j increases, index k-j decreases. So row segment is f[k - (end-1)] to f[k-start] reversed? simpler loop k and use f[k - np.arange(jmin,jmax)] but weights orientation: weight for g_j is f[k-j]. Algorithm mirror descent:
```
```
while time:
   c = conv(a)
   M = max c
   p = softmax(beta*(c-M))
   grad = 2 * cross(a, p)  # as earlier (ar*p)[n-1:n]
   # maybe subtract mean to maintain scale? not needed with normalize
   z = log(a + eps) - lr * grad
   z -= max(z)
   a = exp(z); a/=sum
```


Time budget for Adam:
Define `run_adam(a, n, iterations, beta_start, beta_end, lr, temp_start, temp_end, seed)` returns best a. Use internal deadline? We'll pass iterations or deadline.
Pseudo:
```
def run_adam(a, n, time_limit, beta_steps, lr_start, lr_end, temp_start, temp_end):
    a = normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    loga = np.log(a + eps)
    loga -= loga.max()
    m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_limit
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       # progress frac maybe min(1, it/max_iter)
       frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/time_limit)
       temp = temp_start * (temp_end/temp_start)**frac
       beta = n / max(temp, 1e-4)
       lr = lr_start + (lr_end-lr_start)*frac
       c = fft_conv(a)
       M = np.max(c)
       # weights
       scaled = beta*(c-M)
       scaled -= np.max(scaled) # already 0
       w = np.exp(scaled)
       sw = w.sum()
       if not finite: skip
       p=w/sw
       # optional top threshold
       seg = grad_fft(a,p,n)
       seg -= seg.mean()
       # adam
       m = 0.9*m +0.1*seg
       v=0.99*v+0.01*seg*seg
       step = lr * m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
       loga -= step
       loga -= loga.max()
       a_new = np.exp(loga)
       a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0, 1e6)
       s=a_new.sum()
       if s<=0: break
       a_new/=s
       score=fast_score(a_new)
       if score<best_score:
           best=a_new; best_score=score; if print...
       a=a_new
       # occasional if score exact? use best after
    return best,best_score
```

Implementation details for FFT conv:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```
For x=1? If n>1. `1 << (x-1).bit_length()`.
```
def fft_convolution_square(a):
    n = len(a)
    L = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L, n=L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
`irfft` with `n=L`.
For gradient cross:
```
def fft_cross_shift(a,p):
    n=len(a)
    L = next_pow2(3*n-2)
    ar = a[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,L); fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp,L)
    return 2.0*conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Check p length 2n-1; rfft pads zeros.

Fast score: Since a normalized sum1, `c=fft_conv_square(a); maxc = float(np.max(np.maximum(c,0))); return 2.0*n*maxc`. But for non-normalized? We'll normalize always. For exact candidate, evaluate_sequence may clamp/cut; but our a nonneg <=1000; sum1 OK. If a has zeros, fine.

To evaluate many times, precompute W shape (n,K) where each row two nonzero for linear interpolation. But if sample exactly at knots. Build:
```
W=np.zeros((n,K))
for i,x in enumerate(xs):
   if x<=0: W[i,0]=1
   elif x>=1: W[i,K-1]=1
   else:
      idx = int(np.floor(x*K))  # 0..K-1
      if idx>=K-1: idx=K-2
      frac = x*K - idx
      W[i, idx] = 1-frac
      W[i, idx+1] = frac
```
Vectorized:
```
idx = np.minimum((xs*K).astype(int), K-2)
frac = xs*K - idx
W = np.zeros((n,K)); np.add.at(W, (np.arange(n), idx), 1-frac); np.add.at(W, (np.arange(n), idx+1), frac)
```
If K=1, uniform. K>=2.

Piecewise score objective:
```
def piecewise_de_objective(v):
    s = _DE_W @ v
    tot=s.sum()
    if tot <= 1e-12 or not finite: return 1e6
    a = s/tot
    return fast_score(a)
```

```
def resize_sequence(a, new_n):
    old_n=len(a)
    xs_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n); xs_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    b=np.interp(xs_new, xs_old, a)
    b=np.clip(b,0,1000); b/=sum
    return b
```
For very sparse a, interp okay.

Helpers:
```
def next_power_of_two(x):
    if x <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```
`int.bit_length` ok.

```
def normalize_nonnegative(x, floor=0.0):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim!=1 or x.size==0: return None
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1000.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.ones_like(x)/x.size
    return x/s
```
But if we floor small, sum. In search, keep positive.

`fft_conv_sq(a)`:
```
def fft_conv_sq(a):
   n=len(a); L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)
   c=c[:2*n-1]
   # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
   # numerical negatives
   return c
```
We might not want to clip c before max because if all true negative? impossible. Use `np.max(c)`. Negative tiny not issue.

`fast_score(a)`:
```
def fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12: return 1e100
   a=a/s
   c=fft_conv_sq(a)
   m=float(np.max(c))
   if m < 0: m=0.0
   return float(2.0*len(a)*m)
```
For exact nonnegative sum1, m>=0.

`fft_grad(a,p)`:
```
def fft_grad(a,p):
   n=len(a); L=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
   ar=a[::-1].copy()
   fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,L)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp,L)
   return 2.0*conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Need ensure p length = 2n-1; if not, pad. We'll pass correct.

`soft_weights(c,beta)`:
```
def softmax_weights(c,beta):
   M=float(np.max(c))
   z=beta*(c-M)
   # z max 0
   # if beta large, z very negative; exp underflow okay, but if M not finite?
   z=np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0) # avoid underflow warnings
   w=np.exp(z)
   sw=w.sum()
   if sw <=0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
       w=np.zeros_like(z); w[np.argmax(c)]=1.0
   else: w/=sw
   return w
```
Clipping at -745 (min exp double) to avoid underflow; but if many clipped to 0 except max, fine.

`run_adam`:
Need handle `budget_s` maybe 0.
```
def run_adam(init, n, budget_s, temp_start=5.0,temp_end=0.05,lr_start=0.3,lr_end=0.02, seed=0, print_tag=''):
   a = normalize_nonnegative(interpolate_to_len(init,n) if init is not None else np.ones(n))
   if a is None: a=np.ones(n)/n
   best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
   loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   loga-=loga.max()
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); it=0
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0.0,budget_s)
   print(f"{print_tag} start score {best_score:.6f}")
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       elapsed=max(1e-9, time.monotonic()-start)
       frac=min(1.0, elapsed/max(1e-9,budget_s))
       temp = temp_start * ((temp_end/temp_start) ** frac)
       beta = n / max(temp, 1e-6)
       lr = lr_start + (lr_end-lr_start)*frac
       c=fft_conv_sq(a)
       M=float(np.max(c))
       p=softmax_weights(c,beta)
       g=fft_grad(a,p)
       g-=float(np.mean(g))
       # if g all zero? maybe if a uniform & p uniform, m decays? no move. add tiny random perturbation every 1000 if no improvement.
       m = 0.9*m + 0.1*g
       v = 0.99*v + 0.01*(g*g)
       b1=1-0.9**it; b2=1-0.99**it
       step = lr * (m/b1) / (np.sqrt(v/b2)+1e-8)
       loga -= step
       loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 50.0)
       loga -= loga.max()
       newa=np.exp(loga)
       newa=np.where(np.isfinite(newa), newa, 0.0)
       ns=newa.sum()
       if ns <= 1e-12:
           a=best.copy(); continue
       newa/=ns
       sc=fast_score(newa)
       if sc < best_score:
           best=newa.copy(); best_score=sc
           if sc < 1.52: print tag score...
           # maybe save logs
       a=newa
   return best,best_score
```
Potential: If initial is uniform, p uniform, g constant -> step 0 after center, stuck. Need add random perturbation if no move. We can initialize with small random noise always. In run_adam, after normalize, add relative noise: `a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)); normalize`. But for deterministic maybe. Use rng seed. This helps escape uniform. At each run, pass a pre-perturbed init. Or inside: if g max<1e-12 for some it, perturb loga with noise. Simpler: before run, `a = normalize(rng.uniform?`? For parent uniform, we can mix with random: `a = normalize(0.9*parent + 0.1*random)`.


`interpolate_to_len(seq, new_n)`:
```
def interpolate_to_len(a, new_n):
   old_n=len(a)
   if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
   if old_n < 2 or new_n < 2: ...
   xold=np.linspace(0,1,old_n, endpoint=True)
   xnew=np.linspace(0,1,new_n, endpoint=True)
   b=np.interp(xnew, xold, np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64))
   b=np.clip(b,0,1000)
   s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s
   return b
```
Using endpoint=True for density? Sequence endpoints correspond t=0 and1. OK.

`make_fourier_init(n, rng, kind)`:
```
def make_fourier_init(n, rng, strength_scale=1.0):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    raw=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,9):
       amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0/(k)) * strength_scale
       phi = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       if rng.random()<0.5: raw += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+phi)
       else: raw += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x+phi)
    if rng.random()<0.7: raw += rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)*(2*x-1)*(1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1)
    raw = rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5) + raw
    a=np.exp(np.clip(raw,-3,3))
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)
```
Maybe raw with exp creates high variation; okay. We can also use tanh bounded:
```
a = 1 + 0.9*np.tanh(raw)
```
Which is better? Tanh keeps positive near uniform, exp can create spikes. Use both: choose based on rng.

```
if rng.random()<0.5: a=np.exp(clip(raw,-2,2)) else a=1+0.9*tanh(raw)
```
Then normalize.

Piecewise search:

```
_DE_W = None
piecewise_de_objective(v):
    # v length K
    y=_DE_W @ v
    s=y.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return 1e100
    a=y/s
    sc=fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return 1e100
    return sc
```
Build W:
```
def build_interp_weights(n, K):
    if K < 2: return np.ones((n,1))/n? but K>=2
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    idx=np.floor(xs*(K-1)).astype(int)
    idx=np.clip(idx,0,K-2)
    frac=xs*(K-1)-idx
    W=np.zeros((n,K), dtype=np.float64)
    row=np.arange(n)
    np.add.at(W,(row,idx),1.0-frac)
    np.add.at(W,(row,idx+1),frac)
    return W
```
For x=1, floor(K-1) maybe K-1, clip K-2, frac=1, weight on K-1 0, K-1 1. Good.

`simple_piecewise_search`:
```
def simple_piecewise_search(n,K,budget_s,seed):
   global _DE_W
   W=build_interp_weights(n,K); _DE_W=W
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   best_v = 1.0 + rng.normal(0,0.3,K); best_v=clip
   best_sc = piecewise_de_objective(best_v)
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # CMA-like mutation
       scale = 0.3
       cand = best_v.copy()
       mask = rng.random(K) < 0.5
       cand[mask] += rng.normal(0,scale, int(mask.sum()))
       cand = np.clip(cand,0,1)
       sc=piecewise_score(cand)
       if sc<best_sc: best_v=cand; best_sc=sc
       # occasionally large jump
       if rng.random()<0.05:
           cand = rng.uniform(0,1,K)
           sc=...
   return best_sc, normalize(W@best_v)
```

```
    # allocate times e.g. 0.4,0.3,0.3 of budget

             if res and res.x is not None:
```

`normalize_nonnegative`: If x from W@v all zeros? Return uniform same length? We need know length. It can return `np.ones_like(x)/x.size`.
```
def normalize_nonnegative(x, fallback_uniform=True):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0: return np.ones(1)
    x[np.isnan(x)]? Use `x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)`
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones_like(x) / float(x.size)
    return x/s
```

`interpolate_to_len`:
```
def interpolate_to_len(a, new_n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_n=a.size
    if new_n <=0: return np.ones(1)
    if old_n <=0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    if old_n == new_n:
       return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if old_n ==1:
       return np.full(new_n, float(a[0])/new_n)
    xold=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    xnew=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    b=np.interp(xnew,xold,a)
    return normalize_nonnegative(b)
```

`make_fourier_init`:
```
def make_fourier_init(n, rng, max_harmonic=8):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    raw=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, max_harmonic+1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0/float(k))
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            raw += amp * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*x + phase)
        else:
            raw += amp * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*k*x + phase)
    if rng.random() < 0.8:
        raw += rng.uniform(0.3, 1.2) * (2.0*x - 1.0) * (1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0)
    raw += rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3)
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        a = np.exp(np.clip(raw, -3.0, 3.0))
    else:
        a = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.tanh(raw)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)
```

`make_ramp_init`:
```
def make_ramp_init(n, sign, strength=1.5):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    a=1.0 + 0.9*np.tanh(strength*sign*(2*x-1))
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)
```

`build_interp_weights` as above. Ensure W normalized? It interpolates values; not normalized.

`piecewise_de_objective`:
```
def piecewise_de_objective(v):
    W = _DE_W
    if W is None: return 1e100
    y = W @ np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12: return 1e100
    a = y/s
    return fast_score(a)
```

`simple_piecewise_search`:
```
def simple_piecewise_search(n, K, budget_s, seed):
    global _DE_W
    W = build_interp_weights(n, K)
    _DE_W = W
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_v = rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,K)
    best_sc = piecewise_de_objective(best_v)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0.0,budget_s)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       # mutate best
       scale = 0.25
       mask = rng.random(K) < 0.6
       cand = best_v.copy()
       if np.any(mask):
           cand[mask] += rng.normal(0.0, scale, int(np.sum(mask)))
       cand = np.clip(cand,0.0,1.0)
       sc = piecewise_de_objective(cand)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_v=cand.copy()
       # occasional random restart
       if it % 100 ==0 and rng.random()<0.2:
           cand = rng.uniform(0,1,K)
           sc=...
           if sc<best_sc: update
       # occasional smooth bump
       if it % 200 ==0:
           center = rng.random(K)
           width = rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)
           bump = 0.5*np.exp(-0.5*((np.arange(K)/(K-1)-center)/width)**2)
           cand=np.clip(0.8*best_v + 0.2*bump,0,1)
           sc=...
    a=normalize_nonnegative(W @ best_v)
    return best_sc,a
```
This uses W in objective. Good.

`piecewise_search`:
```
def piecewise_search(budget_s, n, seed):
    start = time.monotonic(); deadline = start + max(0.0,budget_s)
    best_score = 1e100; best_a = None
    configs = [(12,80,12),(20,70,14),(30,60,16)]
    # maybe time split by configs
    for idx,(K,maxiter,pop) in enumerate(configs):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline or K>n: break
       # for small n, K <= n? if K>n set K=n
       Kuse=min(K,n)
       done=False
       if _HAVE_SCIPY:
           try:
              _DE_W = build_interp_weights(n,Kuse)
              bounds=[]
              for i in range(Kuse): bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
              # allocate small fraction maxiter if low dims? use fixed
              res=_scipy_optimize.differential_evolution(piecewise_de_objective, bounds, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=pop, polish=False, seed=seed+Kuse, tol=1e-4, init='latinhypercube')
              if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                  sc=float(res.fun)
                  a=normalize_nonnegative(_DE_W @ res.x)
                  if sc < best_score:
                     best_score=sc; best_a=a
                  done=True
           except Exception:
              done=False
       if not done:
          # use remaining time for this K maybe 20s or half
          rem=max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
          use_t=min(45.0, 0.25*budget_s)  # not too much
          if use_t <=0: use_t = min(15.0, rem)
          sc,a = simple_piecewise_search(n,Kuse,use_t,seed+Kuse)
          if sc < best_score: update
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
    return best_a,best_score
```

Build A efficiently:
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
   n=len(f)
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for j in range(n):
       A[j:j+n, j] = f
   return A
```

`lp_polish` detail:
```
def lp_polish(a, budget_s, n_lp=512, seed=0):
    f = interpolate_to_len(a, n_lp)
    best=f.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(f)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s
    it=0
    biases = [np.linspace(0,1,n_lp), np.sin(2π*...), rng?]
    while time<deadline:
       it+=1
       c=fft_conv_sq(f); M=float(np.max(c)); best_sc_f=2nM
       if M <=0: break
       A=build_conv_matrix(f)  # expensive each iter; could update only when f changes. n=512 ok.
       bias = biases[it % len(biased)]
       c_obj = -np.ones(n_lp) - 1e-5 * bias
       try:
          res = _scipy_optimize.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n_lp, method='highs')
       except: break
       if not res.success: continue
       g=res.x; g=np.clip(g,0,1000); s=g.sum(); if s<=1e-12: continue
       h=g/s
       # mix
       h = (1-mix)*h + mix*f; h=normalize_nonnegative(h)
       # line search
       improved=False
       for t in [1.0,0.75,0.5,0.33,0.2,0.1,0.05]:
          nf=(1-t)*f + t*h; nf=normalize_nonnegative(nf)
          sc=fast_score(nf)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best=nf; best_sc=sc; f=nf; improved=True; break? choose best t not break if want min.
       # if no improvement, try t smaller? we did.
       if not improved and it>10: maybe break or random restart? Break.
       print
    return best,best_sc
```

Check `next_power_of_two`: for x integer. If x is np.int? Use int.
```
def next_power_of_two(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()
```

`fft_conv_sq`:
```
def fft_conv_sq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    L = next_power_of_two(2*n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
`fa*fa` complex square? For convolution of a with a, should be `fa * fa` because same signal (not conjugate). Since a real, fa complex; product not |fa|^2. That's correct for linear convolution of a with a (not autocorrelation). Using `fa*fa`. If a real, fa may be complex; product can have imaginary? FFT convolution theorem: IFFT(FFT(a)*FFT(a)) gives convolution. Yes. `fa*fa` not abs. Good. If we used autocorrelation would be fa*conj. Here np.convolve(a,a) is convolution, same. Since kernel same, no reversal, product. Good.

- `bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n_lp` OK.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_optimize
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_DE_W = None


def next_power_of_two(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonnegative(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return x / s


def interpolate_to_len(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_n = a.size
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]) / float(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(xnew, xold, a)
    return normalize_nonnegative(b)


def fft_conv_sq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if a.size == 0 or s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100
    a = a / s
    c = fft_conv_sq(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        m = 0.0
    z = beta * (c - m)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / sw
    return w


def fft_grad(a, p):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1].copy()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, L)
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def build_conv_matrix(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = f.size
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = f
    return A


def candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def make_ramp_init(n, sign, strength=1.5):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.tanh(strength * float(sign) * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def make_fourier_init(n, rng, max_harmonic=8):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, int(max_harmonic) + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0 / float(k))
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            raw += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x + phase)
        else:
            raw += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x + phase)
    if rng.random() < 0.8:
        scale = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.2)
        direction = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        raw += scale * direction * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    raw += rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        a = np.exp(np.clip(raw, -3.0, 3.0))
    else:
        a = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.tanh(raw)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def build_interp_weights(n, K):
    if K <= 1:
        return np.ones((n, 1), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = xs * float(K - 1)
    idx = np.floor(pos).astype(np.int64)
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, K - 2)
    frac = pos - idx
    W = np.zeros((n, K), dtype=np.float64)
    rows = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    np.add.at(W, (rows, idx), 1.0 - frac)
    np.add.at(W, (rows, idx + 1), frac)
    return W


def piecewise_de_objective(v):
    W = _DE_W
    if W is None:
        return 1e100
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = W @ v
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100
    a = y / s
    return fast_score(a)


def simple_piecewise_search(n, K, budget_s, seed):
    global _DE_W
    W = build_interp_weights(n, K)
    _DE_W = W
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    K = max(2, K)
    best_v = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K)
    best_sc = piecewise_de_objective(best_v)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        mask = rng.random(K) < 0.6
        cand = best_v.copy()
        if np.any(mask):
            cand[mask] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, int(np.sum(mask)))
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0)
        sc = piecewise_de_objective(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_v = cand.copy()

        if it % 100 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.25:
            cand = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K)
            sc = piecewise_de_objective(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_v = cand.copy()

        if it % 150 == 0:
            center = rng.random()
            width = rng.uniform(0.15, 0.5)
            knots = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K, dtype=np.float64)
            bump = 0.6 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((knots - center) / width) ** 2)
            cand = np.clip(0.75 * best_v + 0.25 * bump, 0.0, 1.0)
            sc = piecewise_de_objective(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_v = cand.copy()

    a = normalize_nonnegative(W @ best_v)
    return float(best_sc), a


def piecewise_search(budget_s, n, seed):
    global _DE_W
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    best_score = 1e100
    best_a = None

    configs = [(16, 110, 14), (28, 90, 16)]
    for K, maxiter, popsize in configs:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        Kuse = max(4, min(int(K), int(n)))
        done = False
        if _HAVE_SCIPY:
            try:
                _DE_W = build_interp_weights(n, Kuse)
                bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(Kuse)]
                res = _scipy_optimize.differential_evolution(
                    piecewise_de_objective,
                    bounds,
                    maxiter=maxiter,
                    popsize=popsize,
                    polish=False,
                    seed=seed + Kuse,
                    tol=1e-4
                )
                if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                    sc = float(res.fun)
                    a = normalize_nonnegative(_DE_W @ res.x)
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a
                    done = True
            except Exception:
                done = False
        if not done:
            remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
            use_t = min(45.0, max(5.0, 0.20 * float(budget_s)))
            if use_t > remaining:
                use_t = max(5.0, remaining)
            if use_t > 0.0:
                sc, a = simple_piecewise_search(n, Kuse, use_t, seed + Kuse)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a

    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if remaining > 10.0:
            sc, a = simple_piecewise_search(n, 36, min(45.0, remaining), seed + 999)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def run_adam(init, n, budget_s, temp_start=6.0, temp_end=0.04,
             lr_start=0.35, lr_end=0.03, seed=1, tag="adam"):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    if init is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = normalize_nonnegative(interpolate_to_len(init, n))
    if a.size != n:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    loga -= np.max(loga)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    it = 0
    it_since_imp = 0
    last_print = start

    print(f"[{tag}] n={n} start score={best_score:.8f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = fft_conv_sq(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
            M = 0.0
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best = a.copy()
            it_since_imp = 0
            if time.monotonic() - last_print > 15.0:
                print(f"[{tag}] it={it} best={best_score:.8f}")
                last_print = time.monotonic()
        else:
            it_since_imp += 1

        if it_since_imp > 3500:
            a = best.copy()
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            loga -= np.max(loga)
            loga += rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n)
            loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 50.0)
            loga -= np.max(loga)
            it_since_imp = 0
            continue

        elapsed = max(1e-9, time.monotonic() - start)
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, float(budget_s)))
        temp = temp_start * ((temp_end / temp_start) ** frac)
        beta = float(n) / max(temp, 1e-6)
        lr = lr_start + (lr_end - lr_start) * frac

        p = softmax_weights(c, beta)
        g = fft_grad(a, p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))

        m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * g
        v = 0.99 * v + 0.01 * (g * g)
        step = lr * m / (np.sqrt(v) + 1e-8)
        loga -= step
        loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 50.0)
        loga -= np.max(loga)

        newa = np.exp(loga)
        newa = np.where(np.isfinite(newa), newa, 0.0)
        ns = float(np.sum(newa))
        if ns <= 1e-12:
            a = best.copy()
            it_since_imp += 50
            continue
        newa = newa / ns
        a = newa

    return best.copy(), float(best_score)


def lp_polish(a, budget_s, n_lp=256, seed=1):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        a = normalize_nonnegative(interpolate_to_len(a, n_lp))
        return a, fast_score(a)

    f = normalize_nonnegative(interpolate_to_len(a, n_lp))
    n = f.size
    best = f.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(f)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    mix = 0.5

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    biases = [
        np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64),
        np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, n, dtype=np.float64),
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        -np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.sin(np.pi * x),
        1.0 - np.sin(np.pi * x),
    ]

    print(f"[lp] n={n} start score={best_score:.8f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = fft_conv_sq(f)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = build_conv_matrix(f)
        bias = biases[it % len(biases)]
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) - 1e-4 * bias
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        try:
            res = _scipy_optimize.linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=M,
                bounds=bounds
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
        gs = float(np.sum(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs <= 1e-12:
            break

        h = g / gs
        h = (1.0 - mix) * h + mix * f
        h = normalize_nonnegative(h)

        accepted = False
        best_t = None
        best_nf = None
        best_nf_score = best_score

        for t in [1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.333, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05]:
            nf = (1.0 - t) * f + t * h
            nf = normalize_nonnegative(nf)
            sc = fast_score(nf)
            if sc < best_nf_score:
                best_nf_score = sc
                best_nf = nf
                best_t = t

        if best_nf is not None and best_t is not None:
            f = best_nf
            if best_nf_score < best_score:
                best_score = best_nf_score
                best = f.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            accepted = True
            if time.monotonic() - start > 0.0:
                print(f"[lp] it={it} theta={best_t:.3f} score={best_score:.8f}")

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve >= 6:
            break

    return best.copy(), float(best_score)


def exact_score(seq):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in seq]
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            return sc
    except Exception:
        pass
    return 1e100


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    total_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 40.0
    deadline = total_start + max(60.0, total_budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    candidates = []

    # Safe short fallback, score 16/9 ~ 1.777...
    tiny = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    candidates.append((fast_score(tiny), tiny.copy()))

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        pa = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        pa = np.where(np.isfinite(pa), pa, 0.0)
        pa = np.clip(pa, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if pa.size > 0 and pa.sum() > 1e-12:
            parent_arr = normalize_nonnegative(pa)
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    # Stage 1: low-dimensional global piecewise search.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25.0:
        pw_budget = min(120.0, max(15.0, 0.16 * rem))
        pw_a, pw_sc = piecewise_search(pw_budget, 1024, seed)
        if pw_a is not None:
            candidates.append((float(pw_sc), pw_a.copy()))
        candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
        if len(candidates) > 30:
            candidates = candidates[:30]

    # Stage 2: LP polishing on promising small shapes.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if _HAVE_SCIPY and rem > 40.0:
        lp_budget_total = min(100.0, max(20.0, 0.14 * rem))
        lp_inputs = []
        if candidates:
            lp_inputs.append(candidates[0][1])
        if parent_arr is not None:
            lp_inputs.append(parent_arr)
        lp_inputs.append(make_ramp_init(256, 1.0, 1.8))
        lp_inputs.append(make_ramp_init(256, -1.0, 1.8))
        lp_inputs.append(make_fourier_init(256, rng, 8))

        scored_inputs = []
        for a_in in lp_inputs:
            if a_in is None:
                continue
            a_in = normalize_nonnegative(interpolate_to_len(a_in, 256))
            sc = fast_score(a_in)
            scored_inputs.append((sc, a_in))
        scored_inputs.sort(key=candidate_key)

        per_lp = max(8.0, lp_budget_total / max(1.0, float(len(scored_inputs))))
        for i, (_, a_in) in enumerate(scored_inputs[:5]):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem <= 15.0:
                break
            use_lp = min(per_lp, rem - 10.0)
            if use_lp <= 5.0:
                break
            la, ls = lp_polish(a_in, use_lp, n_lp=256, seed=seed + 300 + i)
            if la is not None:
                candidates.append((float(ls), la.copy()))
        candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
        if len(candidates) > 40:
            candidates = candidates[:40]

    # Stage 3: Adam restarts in medium length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0:
        n_restart = 2048
        restart_budget_total = min(360.0, max(90.0, 0.28 * rem))

        inits = []
        for _, a_cand in candidates[:8]:
            inits.append(interpolate_to_len(a_cand, n_restart))
        if parent_arr is not None:
            inits.append(interpolate_to_len(parent_arr, n_restart))
        inits.append(make_ramp_init(n_restart, 1.0, 1.8))
        inits.append(make_ramp_init(n_restart, -1.0, 1.8))
        for i in range(12):
            inits.append(make_fourier_init(n_restart, rng, 8))

        scored_inits = []
        for a_init in inits:
            if a_init is None:
                continue
            a_init = normalize_nonnegative(a_init)
            sc = fast_score(a_init)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored_inits.append((sc, a_init))
        scored_inits.sort(key=candidate_key)

        num_restarts = min(7, max(1, len(scored_inits)))
        per_restart = max(15.0, restart_budget_total / float(num_restarts))
        for i in range(num_restarts):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem <= 35.0:
                break
            a_init = scored_inits[i][1].copy()
            a_init = normalize_nonnegative(a_init * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n_restart)))
            use_r = min(per_restart, rem - 25.0)
            ra, rs = run_adam(
                a_init,
                n_restart,
                use_r,
                temp_start=6.0,
                temp_end=0.05,
                lr_start=0.35,
                lr_end=0.03,
                seed=seed + 500 + i,
                tag=f"restart{i}"
            )
            candidates.append((float(rs), ra))
            candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
            if len(candidates) > 50:
                candidates = candidates[:50]

    # Stage 4: long refinement at larger length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 260.0 and candidates:
        candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
        n_long = 4096
        long_budget = max(240.0, rem - 320.0)
        a_init = candidates[0][1].copy()
        a_init = normalize_nonnegative(interpolate_to_len(a_init, n_long))
        a_init = normalize_nonnegative(a_init * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n_long)))
        la, ls = run_adam(
            a_init,
            n_long,
            long_budget,
            temp_start=4.0,
            temp_end=0.035,
            lr_start=0.30,
            lr_end=0.02,
            seed=seed + 900,
            tag="long4096"
        )
        candidates.append((float(ls), la))
        candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
        if len(candidates) > 30:
            candidates = candidates[:30]

    # Stage 5: optional final upsample/refine near the parent length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 280.0 and candidates:
        n_big_req = 6962
        if parent_arr is not None and 4096 <= parent_arr.size <= 12000:
            n_big_req = int(parent_arr.size)
        n_big_req = max(4096, min(8192, n_big_req))
        best_score_now = candidates[0][0]
        if best_score_now < 1.56:
            a_init = candidates[0][1].copy()
            a_init = normalize_nonnegative(interpolate_to_len(a_init, n_big_req))
            a_init = normalize_nonnegative(a_init * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.06, n_big_req)))
            big_budget = max(120.0, rem - 60.0)
            ba, bs = run_adam(
                a_init,
                n_big_req,
                big_budget,
                temp_start=3.0,
                temp_end=0.03,
                lr_start=0.25,
                lr_end=0.018,
                seed=seed + 1200,
                tag="big"
            )
            candidates.append((float(bs), ba))
            candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)

    # Final exact validation.
    candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
    exact_best = 1e100
    exact_seq = None
    evaluated = 0
    for sc, a in candidates:
        rem = total_start + total_budget - 8.0 - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 3.0:
            break
        if evaluated >= 12:
            break
        a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
        es = exact_score(a)
        evaluated += 1
        print(f"exact candidate fast={sc:.8f} exact={es:.8f} n={a.size}")
        if es < exact_best:
            exact_best = es
            exact_seq = a

    if exact_seq is None:
        exact_seq = candidates[0][1] if candidates else tiny

    out_vals = []
    for x in exact_seq:
        y = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(y):
            y = 0.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        elif y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        out_vals.append(y)

    s = float(sum(out_vals))
    if s <= 0.01:
        out_vals = [1.0]

    print("final best exact:", exact_best)
    return out_vals
```
